| E-003 | 2026-11-15 | **The first family declared before anything of it is built or read (the handoff's section 7 step 3): F-01, the resting-order level atlas (family RL), `prereg/F-01_resting_order_level_atlas.md` sha256 `535033f2dac968b1e76245e53c73368e274dfa5016cfb4b4c6ce58a79288f347` (of its LF text).** Program family k = 10, one-sided level 0.05 / 110. **The grid (20 cells, each counted):** five level kinds known before the order goes live (the prior regular session's high and low; the overnight high and low; the 30-minute opening range's high and low; the prior close; the nearest 100-point round numbers) x two side rules (`break`: a stop one tick beyond the level; `fade`: a limit at the level, filled only on a trade-through by a tick) x two stops (25 and 37.5 points, the registered coin rungs), read at R 1, 1.5 and 3 (the table's three asks); tickets published at least five minutes before they go live, one fill a ticket a day, void when already through at live time, cancelled at the cell's cutoff; the walk conservative (stop first on a two-barrier bar; on the fill bar a stop touch counts and a target touch does not; gaps fill at the open). **The statistic:** the lift over the coin twin at the same fills, computed exactly as half the difference between the rule's trade and its mirror (the coin's expectation, without its noise); a seeded coin twin beside. **Gates before any outcome** (choice block 2020-2023, the rule side and its mirror pooled): the cutoff rule (latest live time with at most 5 % unresolved at every R), K1 supply (at least 100 fills and 12 a year), K2 resolution (no year above 10 %), K3 realised risk (Stage 2 D-308), K4 fill realism and the limit fill's adverse selection reported; the minimum detectable lift on record before the screen. **Outcomes:** stage B chooses on 2020-2023 by the family-wise maximum studentised lift (21-session block bootstrap, seed 20261901) and the largest valued lift at its lower limit -- valued by the value map's own first-order formula at the place whose table ask is the cell's R; stage C judges the one pick on 2024-01-02 to 2026-07-16: its lift's lower bound at 0.05 / 110 above zero and its valued lift at the 95 % bound at least $500 a year, else the family closes; stage D, only if C holds, adds the pick's tickets to P-16's schedule (first to fill takes the firm's one position) and judges it by Stage 2 D-621 (3) against R\* = P-14, seed 20261903. **Confirmation declared now:** NQ from 2026-07-17, at least six months, not owned (a purchase, the operator's). **Readings and safe defaults recorded:** (a) the family's levels, stops, R values and walk rules are contract arithmetic, the handoff's constraints and conservative fill conventions -- nothing fitted; (b) US regular hours only (Stage 2's hours family found other windows worse, handoff 3.1); (c) the 12.5-point stop is not offered (it loses to costs, Stage 2 D-527); (d) `break` and `fade` at one level fill at nearly the same events and so read nearly opposite lifts -- each is still a counted cell, and the family-wise maximum covers their dependence; (e) the overlap with P-14's own 30-minute breakout (entered at the market five bars late) is named: `or30`'s `break` enters at the level instead; (f) the family-wise test in stage B spends nothing of the program budget, because the judged block decides (Stage 2 D-621 (5): one cell per family reaches judgement). | The handoff sections 2, 2.7, 3.3, 3.4, 4.2, 4.3 and 7 step 3;
